## <a name="general"></a>![Required](https://img.shields.io/badge/Required-blue) Property `Config > general`

**Title:** General Section

|                           |                                                                             |
| ------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Type**                  | `object`                                                                    |
| **Additional properties** | ![Any type: allowed](https://img.shields.io/badge/Any%20type-allowed-green) |

**Description:** Model for the 'general' section.

| Property                                                       | Pattern | Type             | Definition | Title/Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| -------------------------------------------------------------- | ------- | ---------------- | ---------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| + [times](#general_times )                                     | No      | Combination      | -          | Time-Related Entries                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| - [accept_static_namelists](#general_accept_static_namelists ) | No      | boolean          | -          | Allow usage of static namelists as input for the tasks. The namelist should be located in the directory specified by platform.namelists and named with the convention namelist_{kind}_{task} where kind could be surfex or master and task forecast/pgd or similar. For the extraction of namelist see [namelists.md](../docs/namelists.md). |
| - [case](#general_case )                                       | No      | string           | -          | Experiment name                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| - [member_str](#general_member_str )                           | No      | string           | -          | String identifier of a member in the ensemble                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| - [member](#general_member )                                   | No      | integer          | -          | Member number of a member in the ensemble                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| - [cnmexp](#general_cnmexp )                                   | No      | string           | -          | Experiment short name                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| - [loglevel](#general_loglevel )                               | No      | enum (of string) | -          | Logger output level                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| - [e923_optional_sections](#general_e923_optional_sections )   | No      | array of integer | -          | Option to include/exclude part 5 and 6 of e923. Set to '[5,6]' to retain traditional climate files.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| - [surfex](#general_surfex )                                   | No      | boolean          | -          | SURFEX switch                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| - [keep_workdirs](#general_keep_workdirs )                     | No      | boolean          | -          | Do not remove working directories                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| - [csc](#general_csc )                                         | No      | enum (of string) | -          | CSC                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| - [cycle](#general_cycle )                                     | No      | enum (of string) | -          | IAL cycle                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| - [event_type](#general_event_type )                           | No      | enum (of string) | -          | Type of extreme event                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| - [famodel](#general_famodel )                                 | No      | string           | -          | faModelName for handling grib output settings. Needed to specify encodings of ensemble members as well as sub-hourly outputs. Do not replace default name.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| - [initfile](#general_initfile )                               | No      | string           | -          | Initial file for the forecast object. Normally no need to provide unless there are special requirements or the forecast object is used in stand alone mode.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| - [initfile_sfx](#general_initfile_sfx )                       | No      | string           | -          | Initial surfex file for the forecast object. Normally no need to provide unless there are special requirements or the forecast object is used in stand alone mode.                                                                                                                                                                           |
| - [namelist](#general_namelist )                               | No      | object           | -          | Namelist                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| - [output_settings](#general_output_settings )                 | No      | object           | -          | Output Settings                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| - [remove_sections](#general_remove_sections )                 | No      | array            | -          | Sections to remove prior to config merge                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| - [windfarm](#general_windfarm )                               | No      | boolean          | -          | Switch for enabeling wind farm parameterisation                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| - [fullpos_windpower](#general_fullpos_windpower )             | No      | boolean          | -          | Switch for enabeling wind power production in fullpos output. Normally no need to provide but wind power output via full pos is not supported in CY48.                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| - [upd_sst_sic](#general_upd_sst_sic )                         | No      | boolean          | -          | Switch to update SST/SIC during forecast                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| - [surfex_sea_ice](#general_surfex_sea_ice )                   | No      | enum (of string) | -          | Treatment of sea ice in surfex                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

### <a name="general_accept_static_namelists"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > general > accept_static_namelists`

|          |           |
| -------- | --------- |
| **Type** | `boolean` |

**Description:** Allow usage of static namelists as input for the tasks. The namelist should be located in the directory specified by platform.namelists and named with the convention namelist_{kind}_{task} where kind could be surfex or master and task forecast/pgd or similar. For the extraction of namelist see [namelists.md](../docs/namelists.md).

### <a name="general_case"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > general > case`

|             |                |
| ----------- | -------------- |
| **Type**    | `string`       |
| **Default** | `"deode_case"` |

**Description:** Experiment name

| Restrictions                      |                                                                                         |
| --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Must match regular expression** | ```^[^0-9][^.]*$``` [Test](https://regex101.com/?regex=%5E%5B%5E0-9%5D%5B%5E.%5D%2A%24) |

| ----------- | ---------- |

* "wildfire"

### <a name="general_famodel"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > general > famodel`

|          |          |
| -------- | -------- |
| **Type** | `string` |

**Description:** faModelName for handling grib output settings. Needed to specify encodings of ensemble members as well as sub-hourly outputs. Do not replace default name.

### <a name="general_initfile"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > general > initfile`

|          |          |
| -------- | -------- |
| **Type** | `string` |

**Description:** Initial file for the forecast object. Normally no need to provide unless there are special requirements or the forecast object is used in stand alone mode.

### <a name="general_initfile_sfx"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > general > initfile_sfx`

|          |          |
| -------- | -------- |
| **Type** | `string` |

**Description:** Initial surfex file for the forecast object. Normally no need to provide unless there are special requirements or the forecast object is used in stand alone mode.

### <a name="general_namelist"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > general > namelist`

**Title:** Namelist

|                           |                                                                             |
| ------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Type**                  | `object`                                                                    |
| **Additional properties** | ![Any type: allowed](https://img.shields.io/badge/Any%20type-allowed-green) |

**Description:** Namelist settings. Various settings for the forecast namelist that have no other place to go.

| Property                                                        | Pattern | Type   | Definition | Title/Description                                                 |
| --------------------------------------------------------------- | ------- | ------ | ---------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- |
| - [nrazts](#general_namelist_nrazts )                           | No      | string | -          | Reset times for instantaneous fluxes ( e.g. min/max temperature ) |
| - [radiation_frequency](#general_namelist_radiation_frequency ) | No      | string | -          | Time interval for radiation compitations in the model.            |

#### <a name="general_namelist_nrazts"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > general > namelist > nrazts`

|          |          |
| -------- | -------- |
| **Type** | `string` |

**Description:** Reset times for instantaneous fluxes ( e.g. min/max temperature )

#### <a name="general_namelist_radiation_frequency"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > general > namelist > radiation_frequency`

|          |          |
| -------- | -------- |
| **Type** | `string` |

**Description:** Time interval for radiation compitations in the model.

|             |           |
| ----------- | --------- |
| **Type**    | `boolean` |
| **Default** | `true`    |

|             |                    |
| ----------- | ------------------ |
| **Type**    | `enum (of string)` |
| **Default** | `"sice"`           |

**Description:** Treatment of sea ice in surfex

Must be one of:
* "sice"
* "none"

## <a name="suite_control"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > suite_control`

**Title:** Suite control

|                           |                                                                             |
| ------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Type**                  | `object`                                                                    |
| **Additional properties** | ![Any type: allowed](https://img.shields.io/badge/Any%20type-allowed-green) |

**Description:** Suite control options

| Property                                                                       | Pattern | Type             | Definition | Title/Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------- | ---------------- | ---------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| - [suite_definition](#suite_control_suite_definition )                         | No      | string           | -          | Suite definition to play in EcFlow                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| - [do_creategrib_static](#suite_control_do_creategrib_static )                 | No      | boolean          | -          | Activate grib conversion for static files                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| - [do_soil](#suite_control_do_soil )                                           | No      | boolean          | -          | Activate preparation of SOILGRID data as input for PGD                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| - [do_pgd](#suite_control_do_pgd )                                             | No      | boolean          | -          | Activate generation of a PGD file                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| - [mode](#suite_control_mode )                                                 | No      | enum (of string) | -          | Choose a strategy to achieve initial/initial_sfx files (start:  start from interpolated files and then propagate the FG between cycles, restart: start from a previous run and then propagate the FG between cycles, cold_start: always start from interpolated files). |
| - [interpolate_boundaries](#suite_control_interpolate_boundaries )             | No      | boolean          | -          | Activates the boundary interpolation                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| - [do_interpolsstsic](#suite_control_do_interpolsstsic )                       | No      | boolean          | -          | Activates the SST & SIC boundary interpolation                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| - [do_marsprep](#suite_control_do_marsprep )                                   | No      | boolean          | -          | Run marsprep or skip extraction                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| - [do_extractsqlite](#suite_control_do_extractsqlite )                         | No      | boolean          | -          | Activates extraction of selected points to SQLite files for verification.                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| - [do_mergesqlite](#suite_control_do_mergesqlite )                             | No      | boolean          | -          | Activates merging of extracted sqlite files. Requires do_extractsqlite to be true, and number of ensemble members to be greater than 1, to have an effect.                                                                                                              |
| - [create_static_data](#suite_control_create_static_data )                     | No      | boolean          | -          | Activate the generation of PGD and monthly climate files in the suite                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| - [create_time_dependent_suite](#suite_control_create_time_dependent_suite )   | No      | boolean          | -          | Activate the time dependent part of the suite                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| - [split_mars](#suite_control_split_mars )                                     | No      | boolean          | -          | Do marsprep only for one step before c903                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| - [do_cleaning](#suite_control_do_cleaning )                                   | No      | boolean          | -          | Do file cleaning                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| - [n_io_merge](#suite_control_n_io_merge )                                     | No      | integer          | -          | Number of IOmerge tasks                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| - [n_creategrib](#suite_control_n_creategrib )                                 | No      | integer          | -          | Number of CreateGrib tasks                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| - [n_addcalculatedfields](#suite_control_n_addcalculatedfields )               | No      | integer          | -          | Number of AddCalculatedFields tasks                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| - [member_specific_static_data](#suite_control_member_specific_static_data )   | No      | boolean          | -          | Whether to create member specific static data (true) or not                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| - [member_specific_mars_prep](#suite_control_member_specific_mars_prep )       | No      | boolean          | -          | Whether to do member specific marsprep (true) or common marsprep                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| - [mirror_globalDT](#suite_control_mirror_globalDT )                           | No      | boolean          | -          | Mirror globalDT suite (true) and trigger if globalDT is available                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| - [mirror_host_case](#suite_control_mirror_host_case )                         | No      | boolean          | -          | Mirror a host suite and trigger if available.                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| - [mirror_offline](#suite_control_mirror_offline )                             | No      | boolean          | -          | Mirror offlineSFX suite and trigger if offline is available                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| - [max_static_data_tasks](#suite_control_max_static_data_tasks )               | No      | integer          | -          | Limit for max number of monthly tasks, run at same time                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| - [DeodeCleaningSuiteDefinition](#suite_control_DeodeCleaningSuiteDefinition ) | No      | object           | -          | -                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

### <a name="suite_control_mode"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > suite_control > mode`

|          |                    |
| -------- | ------------------ |
| **Type** | `enum (of string)` |

**Description:** Choose a strategy to achieve initial/initial_sfx files (start:  start from interpolated files and then propagate the FG between cycles, restart: start from a previous run and then propagate the FG between cycles, cold_start: always start from interpolated files).

Must be one of:
* "start"
* "restart"
* "cold_start"

| - [bdfile_sst_template](#system_bdfile_sst_template )             | No      | string | -          | Name template for input SST & SIC files of the host model                                                            |
| - [bdfile_template](#system_bdfile_template )                     | No      | string | -          | Name template for input boundary files                                                                               |
| - [sstfile_template](#system_sstfile_template )                   | No      | string | -          | Name template for SST & SIC input boundary files interpolated to the model grid (currently hard-coded in model code) |

| - [bindir](#system_bindir )                                       | No      | string | -          | Location of binaries                                                                                                 |
| - [bdclimdir](#system_bdclimdir )                                 | No      | string | -          | Location of host model climate files used for e927/c903                                                              |
| - [wrk](#system_wrk )                                             | No      | string | -          | Location of date specific working directory                                                                          |
| - [fullpos_config_file](#system_fullpos_config_file )             | No      | string | -          | Path to the fullpos yml config file                                                                                  |
| - [archive_timestamp](#system_archive_timestamp )                 | No      | string | -          | Relative path of date specific archive                                                                               |
| - [archive](#system_archive )                                     | No      | string | -          | Absolute path of date specific archive                                                                               |

| - [global_sfcdir](#system_global_sfcdir )                         | No      | string | -          | Path to SFC files needed for c903, which are not in MARS                                                             |

## <a name="boundaries"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > boundaries`

**Title:** Boundary Section

|                           |                                                                             |
| ------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Type**                  | `object`                                                                    |
| **Additional properties** | ![Any type: allowed](https://img.shields.io/badge/Any%20type-allowed-green) |

**Description:** Settings for control of boundary treatment.

| Property                                                          | Pattern | Type                      | Definition | Title/Description                                                                     |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------- | ---------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| - [bdint](#boundaries_bdint )                                     | No      | string                    | -          | Boundary interval                                                                     |
| - [bdshift](#boundaries_bdshift )                                 | No      | string                    | -          | Shift of boundary initial time. E.g. 'PT3H' would mean use 3H old boundaries          |
| - [bdshift_sfx](#boundaries_bdshift_sfx )                         | No      | string                    | -          | Shift of SURFEX prep input initial time. E.g. 'PT24H' would mean use 24H old file     |
| - [lbc_per_task](#boundaries_lbc_per_task )                       | No      | number                    | -          | Number of LBCs processed in a single task                                             |
| - [max_interpolation_tasks](#boundaries_max_interpolation_tasks ) | No      | number                    | -          | Number of interpolate tasks run in parallel                                           |
| - [bdmodel](#boundaries_bdmodel )                                 | No      | enum (of string)          | -          | Coupling model                                                                        |
| - [bdmodel_sfx](#boundaries_bdmodel_sfx )                         | No      | enum (of string)          | -          | SURFEX prep input file                                                                |
| - [sstmodels](#boundaries_sstmodels )                             | No      | array of enum (of string) | -          | Coupling model for SST & SIC                                                          |
| - [sst_is_lsm](#boundaries_sst_is_lsm )                           | No      | enum (of string)          | -          | Switch for using SST as LSM                                                           |
| - [humi_gp](#boundaries_humi_gp )                                 | No      | boolean                   | -          | Value of TFP_Q%LLGP in domain namelist for c903. True for gridpoint humidity.         |
| - [lam](#boundaries_lam )                                         | No      | object                    | -          | LAM related boundary settings                                                         |
| - [ifs](#boundaries_ifs )                                         | No      | object                    | -          | IFS related settings                                                                  |
| - [bd_has_surfex](#boundaries_bd_has_surfex )                     | No      | boolean                   | -          | Set to true if the host model runs with surfex                                        |
| - [do_slaf](#boundaries_do_slaf )                                 | No      | boolean                   | -          | Set to true if using the SLAF scheme for ensemble member boundary files               |
| - [slaflag](#boundaries_slaflag )                                 | No      | string                    | -          | SLAF shift of boundary initial time. E.g. 'PT3H' would mean use 3H old(er) boundaries |
| - [slafdiff](#boundaries_slafdiff )                               | No      | string                    | -          | SLAF difference of boundary initial times. E.g. 'PT6H' would mean a 6H difference     |
| - [slafk](#boundaries_slafk )                                     | No      | number                    | -          | SLAF difference scaling factor                                                        |

## <a name="gribmodify"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > gribmodify`

**Title:** Section for modify grib

|                           |                                                                             |
| ------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Type**                  | `object`                                                                    |
| **Additional properties** | ![Any type: allowed](https://img.shields.io/badge/Any%20type-allowed-green) |

**Description:** Rules for modifications to be made to the grib output

| Property                                                      | Pattern | Type   | Definition | Title/Description                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | ------- | ------ | ---------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| - [conversions](#gribmodify_conversions )                     | No      | array  | -          | List of file types to be converted                                 |
| - [gribmodify_rules_file](#gribmodify_gribmodify_rules_file ) | No      | string | -          | File specifying the gribmodify rules for the specified conversions |
| - [fullpos.1](#gribmodify_fullpos1 )                          | No      | object | -          | Total rain                                                         |
| - [fullpos.2](#gribmodify_fullpos2 )                          | No      | object | -          | Total snow                                                         |
| - [fullpos.3](#gribmodify_fullpos3 )                          | No      | object | -          | Total precpipation                                                 |
| - [fullpos.4](#gribmodify_fullpos4 )                          | No      | object | -          | 10m wind speed                                                     |
| - [fullpos.5](#gribmodify_fullpos5 )                          | No      | object | -          | 100m wind speed                                                    |
| - [fullpos.6](#gribmodify_fullpos6 )                          | No      | object | -          | 10m gust wind speed                                                |
| - [fullpos.7](#gribmodify_fullpos7 )                          | No      | object | -          | 2m dewpoint temperature                                            |
| - [surfex.1](#gribmodify_surfex1 )                            | No      | object | -          | Leaf area index                                                    |
| - [surfex.2](#gribmodify_surfex2 )                            | No      | object | -          | Albedo                                                             |
| - [surfex.3](#gribmodify_surfex3 )                            | No      | object | -          | Top 20 cm average soil moisture                                    |
| - [surfex.4](#gribmodify_surfex4 )                            | No      | object | -          | Top 100 cm average soil moisture                                   |
| - [surfex.5](#gribmodify_surfex5 )                            | No      | object | -          | Top 20 cm average soil temperature                                 |
| - [surfex.6](#gribmodify_surfex6 )                            | No      | object | -          | Top 100 cm soil temperature                                        |
| - [surfex.7](#gribmodify_surfex7 )                            | No      | object | -          | Root depth soil moisture                                           |
| - [surfex.8](#gribmodify_surfex8 )                            | No      | object | -          | Canopy water                                                       |

## <a name="archiving"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > archiving`

**Title:** Archiving Section, defined in include/archiving.toml

|                           |                                                                             |
| ------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Type**                  | `object`                                                                    |
| **Additional properties** | ![Any type: allowed](https://img.shields.io/badge/Any%20type-allowed-green) |

**Description:** Archiving settings for ATOS to ECFS. Locations, and time intervals to archive for each file type

| Property                               | Pattern | Type   | Definition | Title/Description                          |
| -------------------------------------- | ------- | ------ | ---------- | ------------------------------------------ |
| - [prefix](#archiving_prefix )         | No      | object | -          | Path prefixes                              |
| - [hour](#archiving_hour )             | No      | object | -          | Example settings for the 'ArchiveHour task |
| - [](#archiving_additionalProperties ) | No      | object | -          | -                                          |

### <a name="archiving_prefix"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > archiving > prefix`

**Title:** Path prefixes

|                           |                                                                             |
| ------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Type**                  | `object`                                                                    |
| **Additional properties** | ![Any type: allowed](https://img.shields.io/badge/Any%20type-allowed-green) |

**Description:** Prefix to prepend paths. Like 'ec:/user/ for ecfs on atos or 'user@host:' for scp

| Property                                          | Pattern | Type   | Definition | Title/Description                                                                |
| ------------------------------------------------- | ------- | ------ | ---------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| - [archive_type](#archiving_prefix_archive_type ) | No      | string | -          | Prefix to use for available archive types. Define one per type where applicable. |

#### <a name="archiving_prefix_archive_type"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > archiving > prefix > archive_type`

|          |          |
| -------- | -------- |
| **Type** | `string` |

**Description:** Prefix to use for available archive types. Define one per type where applicable.

### <a name="archiving_hour"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > archiving > hour`

**Title:** Example settings for the 'ArchiveHour task

|                           |                                                                             |
| ------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Type**                  | `object`                                                                    |
| **Additional properties** | ![Any type: allowed](https://img.shields.io/badge/Any%20type-allowed-green) |

#### <a name="archiving_hour_example"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > archiving > hour > example`

**Title:** Example settings for the archiving rules. Any number of rules can be defined to accomodate the needs

|                           |                                                                             |
| ------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Type**                  | `object`                                                                    |
| **Additional properties** | ![Any type: allowed](https://img.shields.io/badge/Any%20type-allowed-green) |

| Property                                      | Pattern | Type        | Definition | Title/Description                                                                                                                             |
| --------------------------------------------- | ------- | ----------- | ---------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| - [active](#archiving_hour_example_active )   | No      | boolean     | -          | Activates the default archiving. Could be boolean or string refering to a boolean in the config file. See the sqlite archiving for an example |
| - [inpath](#archiving_hour_example_inpath )   | No      | string      | -          | Location of input files for archiving                                                                                                         |
| - [outpath](#archiving_hour_example_outpath ) | No      | string      | -          | Relative location of the archived files                                                                                                       |
| - [pattern](#archiving_hour_example_pattern ) | No      | Combination | -          | Search pattern for files to be included. Could be a single pattern or list of patterns.                                                       |
| - [newname](#archiving_hour_example_newname ) | No      | string      | -          | Target name for renaming of files. Only works is pattern is a string and matches an existing file                                             |
| - [tarname](#archiving_hour_example_tarname ) | No      | string      | -          | Tar file name in case of compress method.                                                                                                     |

### <a name="cleaning_defaults"></a>![Required](https://img.shields.io/badge/Required-blue) Property `Config > cleaning > defaults`

|                           |                                                                             |
| ------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Type**                  | `combining`                                                                 |
| **Additional properties** | ![Any type: allowed](https://img.shields.io/badge/Any%20type-allowed-green) |

| Property                                                       | Pattern | Type    | Definition | Title/Description                                                                                                                           |
| -------------------------------------------------------------- | ------- | ------- | ---------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| - [include](#cleaning_defaults_include )                       | No      | string  | -          | regular experssion according to it which files to clean                                                                                     |
| - [ncycles_delay](#cleaning_defaults_ncycles_delay )           | No      | number  | -          | number of cycle_length periods with which the cleaning is shifted to the experiment start day to the past.                                  |
| - [cleaning_delay](#cleaning_defaults_cleaning_delay )         | No      | string  | -          | time period with which the cleaning is shifted to the experiment start day to the past. Should be a multiple of general.times.cycle_length. |
| - [cleaning_max_delay](#cleaning_defaults_cleaning_max_delay ) | No      | string  | -          | time period which defines the cleaning according to the cleaning_delay to the past. Should be a multiple of general.times.cycle_length.     |
| - [step](#cleaning_defaults_step )                             | No      | string  | -          | time step which determines moments when to make a cleaning, should be a multiple of general.times.cycle_length.                             |
| - [active](#cleaning_defaults_active )                         | No      | boolean | -          | Activate cleaning files                                                                                                                     |
| - [dry_run](#cleaning_defaults_dry_run )                       | No      | boolean | -          | test cleaning without to  delete files                                                                                                      |
| - [path](#cleaning_defaults_path )                             | No      | string  | -          | Path to clean                                                                                                                               |
| - [wipe](#cleaning_defaults_wipe )                             | No      | boolean | -          | Flag for cleaning everything in a path                                                                                                      |

| One of(Option)                        |
| ------------------------------------- |
| [item 0](#cleaning_defaults_oneOf_i0) |
| [item 1](#cleaning_defaults_oneOf_i1) |

|            |            |

| - [gridtype_oro](#domain_gridtype_oro )                                     | No      | enum (of string)  | -          | Select truncation type for orographic_smoothing                            |
| - [orographic_smoothing_method](#domain_orographic_smoothing_method )       | No      | enum (of string)  | -          | Orographic smoothing method                                                |
| - [name](#domain_name )                                                     | No      | string            | -          | Name of your domain                                                        |
| - [number](#domain_number )                                                 | No      | Combination       | -          | Sequence number for domain                                                 |
| - [nzsfilter](#domain_nzsfilter )                                           | No      | enum (of integer) | -          | Activate the PGD orographic filtering                                      |
| - [nimax](#domain_nimax )                                                   | No      | number            | -          | Number of grid points zonally                                              |
| - [njmax](#domain_njmax )                                                   | No      | number            | -          | Number of grid points meridionally                                         |
| - [xdx](#domain_xdx )                                                       | No      | number            | -          | Zonal grid size in meters                                                  |
| - [xdy](#domain_xdy )                                                       | No      | number            | -          | Meridional grid size in meters                                             |
| - [ilone](#domain_ilone )                                                   | No      | number            | -          | Zonal extension zone                                                       |
| - [ilate](#domain_ilate )                                                   | No      | number            | -          | Meridional extension zone                                                  |
| - [nbzong](#domain_nbzong )                                                 | No      | number            | -          | Meridional coupling zone width                                             |
| - [nbzonl](#domain_nbzonl )                                                 | No      | number            | -          | Zonal coupling zone width                                                  |
| - [tstep](#domain_tstep )                                                   | No      | Combination       | -          | Model time step in seconds                                                 |
| - [xloncen](#domain_xloncen )                                               | No      | number            | -          | Domain center longitude                                                    |
| - [xlatcen](#domain_xlatcen )                                               | No      | number            | -          | Domain center latitude                                                     |
| - [xlon0](#domain_xlon0 )                                                   | No      | Combination       | -          | Longitude of center of projection                                          |
| - [xlat0](#domain_xlat0 )                                                   | No      | Combination       | -          | Latitude of center of projection                                           |
| - [xbeta](#domain_xbeta )                                                   | No      | number            | -          | Rotation parameter for the conformal projection                            |
| - [spectral_smoothing_by_gridtype](#domain_spectral_smoothing_by_gridtype ) | No      | object            | -          | Defines for which gridypes spectral smoothing is applied when activated    |
| - [truncation_by_gridtype](#domain_truncation_by_gridtype )                 | No      | object            | -          | Defines default gridtype_oro mapping when truncation smoothing is applied. |
| - [dtf_lead_time](#domain_dtf_lead_time )                                   | No      | enum (of string)  | -          | Lead time of DTF, which trigger the domain                                 |
| - [](#domain_additionalProperties )                                         | No      | object            | -          | -                                                                          |

| **Default** | `"FCTABLE/{MODEL}/{YYYY}/{MM}/FCTABLE_{PP}_{YYYY}{MM}_{HH}.sqlite"` |

**Description:** File name template for generated sqlite files

### <a name="extractsqlite_selection"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > extractsqlite > selection`

|             |          |
| ----------- | -------- |
| **Type**    | `string` |
| **Default** | `"PT1H"` |

**Description:** Temporal selection. Same syntax as for output_settings. Leave empty to inherit the fullpos settings.

| - [databridge_user](#fdb_databridge_user )         | No      | string | -          | User allowed to write to the databridge                                                   |
| - [](#fdb_additionalProperties )                   | No      | object | -          | -                                                                                         |

## <a name="file_templates"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > file_templates`

**Title:** File Templates, defined in include/file_templates.toml

|                           |                                                                             |
| ------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Type**                  | `object`                                                                    |
| **Additional properties** | ![Any type: allowed](https://img.shields.io/badge/Any%20type-allowed-green) |

**Description:** Templates for file names. Each item contains of an archive and a model key where archive is the name template for files in the archive and model the name expected as input/output of the IAL code.

| Property                                                              | Pattern | Type   | Definition | Title/Description          |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------- | ------ | ---------- | -------------------------- |
| - [duration](#file_templates_duration )                               | No      | object | -          | Duration                   |
| - [ddh_dl](#file_templates_ddh_dl )                                   | No      | object | -          | DDH limited area or points |
| - [ddh_gl](#file_templates_ddh_gl )                                   | No      | object | -          | Global DDH                 |
| - [ddh_zo](#file_templates_ddh_zo )                                   | No      | object | -          | Zonal DDH                  |
| - [fullpos](#file_templates_fullpos )                                 | No      | object | -          | Fullpos                    |
| - [interpolated_boundaries](#file_templates_interpolated_boundaries ) | No      | object | -          | Interpolated boundaries    |
| - [history](#file_templates_history )                                 | No      | object | -          | History                    |
| - [pgd](#file_templates_pgd )                                         | No      | object | -          | PGD                        |
| - [pgd_prel](#file_templates_pgd_prel )                               | No      | object | -          | Preliminary PGD            |
| - [pgd_host](#file_templates_pgd_host )                               | No      | object | -          | Host PGD                   |
| - [surfex](#file_templates_surfex )                                   | No      | object | -          | Surfex                     |
| - [](#file_templates_additionalProperties )                           | No      | object | -          | -                          |

| - [i8hy](#mars_i8hy )                       | No      | object | -          | AQ winter case over central Europe                    |

### <a name="mars_HRES"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > mars > HRES`

**Title:** Operational HRES

|                           |                                                                             |
| ------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Type**                  | `object`                                                                    |
| **Additional properties** | ![Any type: allowed](https://img.shields.io/badge/Any%20type-allowed-green) |

**Description:** Parameters for oper data

| Property                                           | Pattern | Type   | Definition | Title/Description                                                                                                                                                    |
| -------------------------------------------------- | ------- | ------ | ---------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| - [ifs_cycle_start](#mars_HRES_ifs_cycle_start )   | No      | string | -          | Boundary model cycle start hour. Required to be set if bdcycle_start % bdcycle != 0                                                                                  |
| - [class](#mars_HRES_class )                       | No      | string | -          | Class of expver                                                                                                                                                      |
| - [levelist](#mars_HRES_levelist )                 | No      | object | -          | Dictionary (date:levels), with dates of change in the number of levels.                                                                                              |
| - [grid](#mars_HRES_grid )                         | No      | object | -          | Dictionary (date:resolution), with dates of change in the resolution.                                                                                                |
| - [ifs_cycle_length](#mars_HRES_ifs_cycle_length ) | No      | string | -          | Boundary model cycle interval                                                                                                                                        |
| - [start_date](#mars_HRES_start_date )             | No      | string | -          | Date from when are data available in Mars                                                                                                                            |
| - [GG](#mars_HRES_GG )                             | No      | string | -          | List of parameters for grid point data, for detailed information about the parameters check <a href='https://codes.ecmwf.int/grib/param-db/'>here</a>                |
| - [GG1](#mars_HRES_GG1 )                           | No      | string | -          | List of constant field parameters for grid point data, for detailed information about the parameters check <a href='https://codes.ecmwf.int/grib/param-db/'>here</a> |
| - [GG_sea](#mars_HRES_GG_sea )                     | No      | string | -          | Parameters for grid point sea data, for detailed information about the parameters check <a href='https://codes.ecmwf.int/grib/param-db/'>here</a>                    |
| - [GG_soil](#mars_HRES_GG_soil )                   | No      | string | -          | Parameters for grid point soil data, for detailed information about the parameters check <a href='https://codes.ecmwf.int/grib/param-db/'>here</a>                   |
| - [grid_GG1](#mars_HRES_grid_GG1 )                 | No      | object | -          | Grid parameter for mars request of GG1 fields                                                                                                                        |
| - [grid_ML](#mars_HRES_grid_ML )                   | No      | string | -          | Grid parameter for mars request of model level fields                                                                                                                |
| - [SH](#mars_HRES_SH )                             | No      | string | -          | Parameters for spectral harmonic data, for detailed information about the parameters check <a href='https://codes.ecmwf.int/grib/param-db/'>here</a>                 |
| - [SHZ](#mars_HRES_SHZ )                           | No      | string | -          | Geopotential on ground, for detailed information about the parameters check <a href='https://codes.ecmwf.int/grib/param-db/'>here</a>                                |
| - [UA](#mars_HRES_UA )                             | No      | string | -          | Upper air grid point parameters, for detailed information about the parameters check <a href='https://codes.ecmwf.int/grib/param-db/'>here</a>                       |
| - [SHZ_type](#mars_HRES_SHZ_type )                 | No      | string | -          | Type for geopotential                                                                                                                                                |
| - [GGZ_type](#mars_HRES_GGZ_type )                 | No      | string | -          | Type for geopotential in gridpoint                                                                                                                                   |
| - [resolution](#mars_HRES_resolution )             | No      | string | -          | Resolution of global model                                                                                                                                           |
| - [stream](#mars_HRES_stream )                     | No      | object | -          | Dictionary (run:stream) to use the right stream for a given run.                                                                                                     |

#### <a name="mars_HRES_ifs_cycle_start"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > mars > HRES > ifs_cycle_start`

|             |          |
| ----------- | -------- |
| **Type**    | `string` |
| **Default** | `"PT0H"` |

**Description:** Boundary model cycle start hour. Required to be set if bdcycle_start % bdcycle != 0

| Restrictions                      |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| --------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Must match regular expression** | ```^P(?!$)(\d+Y)?(\d+M)?(\d+W)?(\d+D)?(T(?=\d+[HMS])(\d+H)?(\d+M)?(\d+S)?)?$``` [Test](https://regex101.com/?regex=%5EP%28%3F%21%24%29%28%5Cd%2BY%29%3F%28%5Cd%2BM%29%3F%28%5Cd%2BW%29%3F%28%5Cd%2BD%29%3F%28T%28%3F%3D%5Cd%2B%5BHMS%5D%29%28%5Cd%2BH%29%3F%28%5Cd%2BM%29%3F%28%5Cd%2BS%29%3F%29%3F%24) |

#### <a name="mars_HRES_class"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > mars > HRES > class`

|          |          |
| -------- | -------- |
| **Type** | `string` |

**Description:** Class of expver

#### <a name="mars_HRES_levelist"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > mars > HRES > levelist`

|                           |                                                                             |

| ----------- | -------- |
| **Type**    | `string` |

#### <a name="mars_i5qp_ifs_cycle_start"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > mars > i5qp > ifs_cycle_start`

|             |          |
| ----------- | -------- |
| **Type**    | `string` |
| **Default** | `"PT0H"` |

**Description:** Boundary model cycle start hour. Required to be set if bdcycle_start % bdcycle != 0

| Restrictions                      |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| --------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Must match regular expression** | ```^P(?!$)(\d+Y)?(\d+M)?(\d+W)?(\d+D)?(T(?=\d+[HMS])(\d+H)?(\d+M)?(\d+S)?)?$``` [Test](https://regex101.com/?regex=%5EP%28%3F%21%24%29%28%5Cd%2BY%29%3F%28%5Cd%2BM%29%3F%28%5Cd%2BW%29%3F%28%5Cd%2BD%29%3F%28T%28%3F%3D%5Cd%2B%5BHMS%5D%29%28%5Cd%2BH%29%3F%28%5Cd%2BM%29%3F%28%5Cd%2BS%29%3F%29%3F%24) |

**Description:** Parameters for global DT data

| Property                                              | Pattern | Type             | Definition | Title/Description                                                                                                                                     |
| ----------------------------------------------------- | ------- | ---------------- | ---------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| - [ifs_cycle_start](#mars_lumi_DT_ifs_cycle_start )   | No      | string           | -          | Boundary model cycle start hour. Required to be set if bdcycle_start % bdcycle != 0                                                                   |
| - [default](#mars_lumi_DT_default )                   | No      | string           | -          | Other section to copy settings from if not given here                                                                                                 |
| - [class](#mars_lumi_DT_class )                       | No      | string           | -          | Class of expver                                                                                                                                       |
| - [levelist](#mars_lumi_DT_levelist )                 | No      | string           | -          | Dictionary (date:levels), with dates of change in the number of levels.                                                                               |
| - [grid](#mars_lumi_DT_grid )                         | No      | string           | -          | Dictionary (date:resolution), with dates of change in the resolution.                                                                                 |
| - [ifs_cycle_length](#mars_lumi_DT_ifs_cycle_length ) | No      | string           | -          | Boundary model cycle interval                                                                                                                         |
| - [start_date](#mars_lumi_DT_start_date )             | No      | string           | -          | Date from when are data available in Mars                                                                                                             |
| - [GG](#mars_lumi_DT_GG )                             | No      | string           | -          | List of parameters for grid point data, for detailed information about the parameters check <a href='https://codes.ecmwf.int/grib/param-db/'>here</a> |
| - [GG_sea](#mars_lumi_DT_GG_sea )                     | No      | string           | -          | Parameters for grid point sea data, for detailed information about the parameters check <a href='https://codes.ecmwf.int/grib/param-db/'>here</a>     |
| - [tco](#mars_lumi_DT_tco )                           | No      | enum (of string) | -          | Truncation for static RD data                                                                                                                         |
| - [grid_ML](#mars_lumi_DT_grid_ML )                   | No      | string           | -          | Grid parameter for mars request of model level fields                                                                                                 |
| - [SH](#mars_lumi_DT_SH )                             | No      | string           | -          | Parameters for spectral harmonic data, for detailed information about the parameters check <a href='https://codes.ecmwf.int/grib/param-db/'>here</a>  |
| - [SHZ](#mars_lumi_DT_SHZ )                           | No      | string           | -          | Geopotential on ground, for detailed information about the parameters check <a href='https://codes.ecmwf.int/grib/param-db/'>here</a>                 |
| - [UA](#mars_lumi_DT_UA )                             | No      | string           | -          | Upper air grid point parameters, for detailed information about the parameters check <a href='https://codes.ecmwf.int/grib/param-db/'>here</a>        |
| - [SHZ_type](#mars_lumi_DT_SHZ_type )                 | No      | string           | -          | Type for geopotential                                                                                                                                 |
| - [GGZ_type](#mars_lumi_DT_GGZ_type )                 | No      | string           | -          | Type for geopotential in gridpoint                                                                                                                    |
| - [resolution](#mars_lumi_DT_resolution )             | No      | string           | -          | Resolution of global model                                                                                                                            |
| - [stream](#mars_lumi_DT_stream )                     | No      | Combination      | -          | MARS stream or dictionary (run:stream) to use the right stream for a given run.                                                                       |

#### <a name="mars_lumi_DT_ifs_cycle_start"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > mars > lumi_DT > ifs_cycle_start`

|             |          |
| ----------- | -------- |
| **Type**    | `string` |
| **Default** | `"PT0H"` |

**Description:** Boundary model cycle start hour. Required to be set if bdcycle_start % bdcycle != 0

| Restrictions                      |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| --------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Must match regular expression** | ```^P(?!$)(\d+Y)?(\d+M)?(\d+W)?(\d+D)?(T(?=\d+[HMS])(\d+H)?(\d+M)?(\d+S)?)?$``` [Test](https://regex101.com/?regex=%5EP%28%3F%21%24%29%28%5Cd%2BY%29%3F%28%5Cd%2BM%29%3F%28%5Cd%2BW%29%3F%28%5Cd%2BD%29%3F%28T%28%3F%3D%5Cd%2B%5BHMS%5D%29%28%5Cd%2BH%29%3F%28%5Cd%2BM%29%3F%28%5Cd%2BS%29%3F%29%3F%24) |
