## <a name="platform"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > platform`

**Title:** Platform Section, defined in files under include/platform_paths

|                           |                                                                             |
| ------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Type**                  | `object`                                                                    |
| **Additional properties** | ![Any type: allowed](https://img.shields.io/badge/Any%20type-allowed-green) |

**Description:** DEODE platform specific path settings

| Property                                              | Pattern | Type                      | Definition | Title/Description                                                                              |
| ----------------------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------- | ---------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| - [archive_types](#platform_archive_types )           | No      | array of enum (of string) | -          | Methods available for archiving                                                                |
| - [ecoclim_data_path](#platform_ecoclim_data_path )   | No      | string                    | -          | Path to the ECOCLIMAP-II data                                                                  |
| - [deode_home](#platform_deode_home )                 | No      | string                    | -          | Path to the deode code, normally no need to change                                             |
| - [pgd_data_path](#platform_pgd_data_path )           | No      | string                    | -          | Path to PGD input data                                                                         |
| - [soilgrid_data_path](#platform_soilgrid_data_path ) | No      | string                    | -          | Path to solgrid input data                                                                     |
| - [global_sfcdir](#platform_global_sfcdir )           | No      | string                    | -          | Path to global IFS surface data                                                                |
| - [topo_data_path](#platform_topo_data_path )         | No      | string                    | -          | Path to topography input data directory                                                        |
| - [topo_source](#platform_topo_source )               | No      | enum (of string)          | -          | Topography data source type                                                                    |
| - [ecosg_data_path](#platform_ecosg_data_path )       | No      | string                    | -          | Path to ECOCLIMAP SG data                                                                      |
| - [climdata](#platform_climdata )                     | No      | string                    | -          | Macro used in other platform paths                                                             |
| - [static_data](#platform_static_data )               | No      | string                    | -          | Macro used in other platform paths                                                             |
| - [e923_data](#platform_e923_data )                   | No      | string                    | -          | Path to e923 input data                                                                        |
| - [ncdir](#platform_ncdir )                           | No      | string                    | -          | Path to ECrad NetCDF data                                                                      |
| - [rrtm_dir](#platform_rrtm_dir )                     | No      | string                    | -          | Path to RRTM input data                                                                        |
| - [rttov_coefdir](#platform_rttov_coefdir )           | No      | string                    | -          | Path to RTTOV_COEF input data                                                                  |
| - [scratch](#platform_scratch )                       | No      | string                    | -          | Path used as macro for e.g. wrk and other directories.                                         |
| - [task_name](#platform_task_name )                   | No      | string                    | -          | Placeholder macro.                                                                             |
| - [flake_dir](#platform_flake_dir )                   | No      | string                    | -          | Path to climate PGD dir.                                                                       |
| - [albnir_soil_dir](#platform_albnir_soil_dir )       | No      | string                    | -          | Path to near-infrared spectrum soil data.                                                      |
| - [albnir_veg_dir](#platform_albnir_veg_dir )         | No      | string                    | -          | Path to near-infrared spectrum vegetation data.                                                |
| - [albvis_soil_dir](#platform_albvis_soil_dir )       | No      | string                    | -          | Path to visible soil spectrum data.                                                            |
| - [albvis_veg_dir](#platform_albvis_veg_dir )         | No      | string                    | -          | Path to visible spectrum vegetation data.                                                      |
| - [install_dir](#platform_install_dir )               | No      | string                    | -          | Install location                                                                               |
| - [lai_dir](#platform_lai_dir )                       | No      | string                    | -          | TBA                                                                                            |
| - [osm_data_eu](#platform_osm_data_eu )               | No      | string                    | -          | Directory containing OSM data for Europe                                                       |
| - [tree_height_dir](#platform_tree_height_dir )       | No      | string                    | -          | Path to tree_height data.                                                                      |
| - [windfarm_path](#platform_windfarm_path )           | No      | string                    | -          | Path to wind farm parameterisation input data.                                                 |
| - [eclipse_data_dir](#platform_eclipse_data_dir )     | No      | string                    | -          | Path to sun/moon position as used for eclipses.                                                |
| - [archive_root](#platform_archive_root )             | No      | string                    | -          | Path to the main archive directory                                                             |
| - [unix_group](#platform_unix_group )                 | No      | string                    | -          | Default unix group for directories created by deode, leave empty to use default group of user. |
| - [properties](#platform_properties )                 | No      | object                    | -          | -                                                                                              |
| - [](#platform_additionalProperties )                 | No      | object                    | -          | -                                                                                              |

### <a name="platform_archive_types"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > platform > archive_types`

|             |                                       |
| ----------- | ------------------------------------- |
| **Type**    | `array of enum (of string)`           |
| **Default** | `["compress", "copy", "move", "scp"]` |

**Description:** Methods available for archiving

|                      | Array restrictions |
| -------------------- | ------------------ |
| **Min items**        | N/A                |
| **Max items**        | N/A                |
| **Items unicity**    | False              |
| **Additional items** | False              |
| **Tuple validation** | See below          |

| Each item of this array must be                      | Description |
| ---------------------------------------------------- | ----------- |
| [archive_types items](#platform_archive_types_items) | -           |

#### <a name="platform_archive_types_items"></a>Config > platform > archive_types > archive_types items

|          |                    |
| -------- | ------------------ |
| **Type** | `enum (of string)` |

Must be one of:
* "compress"
* "copy"
* "ecfs"
* "fdb"
* "move"
* "scp"

**Description:** Default unix group for directories created by deode, leave empty to use default group of user.

### <a name="remove_defaults"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > remove > defaults`

|                           |                                                                             |
| ------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Type**                  | `object`                                                                    |
| **Additional properties** | ![Any type: allowed](https://img.shields.io/badge/Any%20type-allowed-green) |

| Property                                                     | Pattern | Type    | Definition | Title/Description                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------------------------------ | ------- | ------- | ---------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| - [include](#remove_defaults_include )                       | No      | string  | -          | regular experssion according to it which files to clean                                                                                     |
| - [ncycles_delay](#remove_defaults_ncycles_delay )           | No      | number  | -          | number of cycle_length periods with which the cleaning is shifted to the experiment start day to the past.                                  |
| - [cleaning_delay](#remove_defaults_cleaning_delay )         | No      | string  | -          | time period with which the cleaning is shifted to the experiment start day to the past. Should be a multiple of general.times.cycle_length. |
| - [cleaning_max_delay](#remove_defaults_cleaning_max_delay ) | No      | string  | -          | time period which defines the cleaning according to the cleaning_delay to the past. Should be a multiple of general.times.cycle_length.     |
| - [step](#remove_defaults_step )                             | No      | string  | -          | time step which determines moments when to make a cleaning, should be a multiple of general.times.cycle_length.                             |
| - [active](#remove_defaults_active )                         | No      | boolean | -          | Activate cleaning files                                                                                                                     |
| - [dry_run](#remove_defaults_dry_run )                       | No      | boolean | -          | test cleaning without to  delete files                                                                                                      |
| - [path](#remove_defaults_path )                             | No      | string  | -          | Path to clean                                                                                                                               |
| - [wipe](#remove_defaults_wipe )                             | No      | boolean | -          | Flag for cleaning everything in a path                                                                                                      |

### <a name="scheduler_mirror_globalDT"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > scheduler > mirror_globalDT`

**Title:** Settings to mirror globalDT

|                           |                                                                             |
| ------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Type**                  | `object`                                                                    |
| **Additional properties** | ![Any type: allowed](https://img.shields.io/badge/Any%20type-allowed-green) |

| Property                                                       | Pattern | Type    | Definition | Title/Description                                                                       |
| -------------------------------------------------------------- | ------- | ------- | ---------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| - [check_var](#scheduler_mirror_globalDT_check_var )           | No      | string  | -          | Remote variable (like YMD) that is checked for triggering. Leave empty if not required. |
| - [mirror_name](#scheduler_mirror_globalDT_mirror_name )       | No      | string  | -          | Name for the mirror in ecflow                                                           |
| - [remote_auth](#scheduler_mirror_globalDT_remote_auth )       | No      | string  | -          | the location to the Mirror authentication credentials file, if required                 |
| - [remote_host](#scheduler_mirror_globalDT_remote_host )       | No      | string  | -          | the remote ecFlow server host                                                           |
| - [remote_path](#scheduler_mirror_globalDT_remote_path )       | No      | string  | -          | the path of the node on the remote ecFlow server                                        |
| - [remote_port](#scheduler_mirror_globalDT_remote_port )       | No      | string  | -          | the remote ecFlow server port                                                           |
| - [remote_polling](#scheduler_mirror_globalDT_remote_polling ) | No      | string  | -          | the value (in seconds) used to periodically contact the remote ecFlow server            |
| - [remote_ssl](#scheduler_mirror_globalDT_remote_ssl )         | No      | boolean | -          | to connect to the ecFlow server using SSL                                               |

## <a name="submission"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > submission`

**Title:** Submission Section, defined in files under include/submission

|                           |                                                                             |
| ------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Type**                  | `object`                                                                    |
| **Additional properties** | ![Any type: allowed](https://img.shields.io/badge/Any%20type-allowed-green) |

**Description:** Defines host dependent runtime settings such as binaries, batch job rules, modules and similar

| Property                                                  | Pattern | Type             | Definition | Title/Description                                                                                                                                        |
| --------------------------------------------------------- | ------- | ---------------- | ---------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| - [account](#submission_account )                         | No      | string           | -          | Global setting of account to be used. E.g. \`#SBATCH -A some_account\` for slurm. Setting of account on group or task level overrides the global setting |
| - [bindir](#submission_bindir )                           | No      | string           | -          | Location of IAL binaries                                                                                                                                 |
| - [bindir_gl](#submission_bindir_gl )                     | No      | string           | -          | Location of GL binary                                                                                                                                    |
| - [compiler](#submission_compiler )                       | No      | enum (of string) | -          | Compiler used to build IAL                                                                                                                               |
| - [precision](#submission_precision )                     | No      | enum (of string) | -          | Floating point precision as used in the Forecast task                                                                                                    |
| - [ial_version](#submission_ial_version )                 | No      | string           | -          | Version of IAL binary to use                                                                                                                             |
| - [gl_version](#submission_gl_version )                   | No      | string           | -          | Version of GL binary to use                                                                                                                              |
| - [nproma](#submission_nproma )                           | No      | number           | -          | NPROMA size in the forecast model. See the namelists for where it is used                                                                                |
| - [lfftw](#submission_lfftw )                             | No      | boolean          | -          | Use of the FFTW library. See the namelists for where it is used                                                                                          |
| - [module_initfile](#submission_module_initfile )         | No      | string           | -          | File to use for module initialization, overrides module_initpath                                                                                         |
| - [module_initpath](#submission_module_initpath )         | No      | string           | -          | Path to the version of env_modules_python.py to be used in the module initialization                                                                     |
| - [default_submit_type](#submission_default_submit_type ) | No      | string           | -          | Defines the default submit type for tasks not listed in any defined type                                                                                 |
| - [task_exceptions](#submission_task_exceptions )         | No      | object           | -          | Allows to define task specific settings like BATCH, MODULES, NPROC. Use as task_exceptions.taskname.property                                             |
| - [types](#submission_types )                             | No      | object           | -          | Submission types                                                                                                                                         |
| - [iomerge](#submission_iomerge )                         | No      | object           | -          | IO-merge group                                                                                                                                           |
| - [](#submission_additionalProperties )                   | No      | object           | -          | -                                                                                                                                                        |

### <a name="submission_account"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > submission > account`

|          |          |
| -------- | -------- |
| **Type** | `string` |

**Description:** Global setting of account to be used. E.g. `#SBATCH -A some_account` for slurm. Setting of account on group or task level overrides the global setting

|             |                                                            |
| ----------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Type**    | `string`                                                   |
| **Default** | `"@INSTALL_DIR@/@CYCLE@/@COMPILER@/R64/@IAL_VERSION@/bin"` |

|             |                                                   |
| ----------- | ------------------------------------------------- |
| **Type**    | `string`                                          |
| **Default** | `"@INSTALL_DIR@/gl/@COMPILER@/@GL_VERSION@/bin/"` |

**Description:** Location of GL binary

* "intel"
* "gnu"
* ""

* ""

#### <a name="submission_types_serial"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > submission > types > serial`

**Title:** Submission group

|                           |                                                                             |
| ------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Type**                  | `object`                                                                    |
| **Additional properties** | ![Any type: allowed](https://img.shields.io/badge/Any%20type-allowed-green) |

**Description:** Defines the properties avilable for a submit group. See the existing submission files for examples

| Property                                         | Pattern | Type            | Definition | Title/Description                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------------ | ------- | --------------- | ---------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| - [tasks](#submission_types_serial_tasks )       | No      | array of string | -          | List of tasks belonging to this group                                                                                           |
| + [SCHOST](#submission_types_serial_SCHOST )     | No      | string          | -          | Name of the host where the job runs                                                                                             |
| - [NPROC](#submission_types_serial_NPROC )       | No      | number          | -          | Number of processors for this task                                                                                              |
| - [NPROC_IO](#submission_types_serial_NPROC_IO ) | No      | number          | -          | Number of io processors for this task, only applicable for tasks involving the forecast model                                   |
| - [WRAPPER](#submission_types_serial_WRAPPER )   | No      | string          | -          | Prefix for binaries to be executed, e.g. srun                                                                                   |
| - [BATCH](#submission_types_serial_BATCH )       | No      | object          | -          | Sets batch job arguments like walltime                                                                                          |
| - [ENV](#submission_types_serial_ENV )           | No      | object          | -          | Sets environment variables                                                                                                      |
| - [MODULES](#submission_types_serial_MODULES )   | No      | object          | -          | Sets modules to purge/use/unload/load or switch. To maintain the order decorate with comments to override the automatic sorting |

| ----------- | -------- |

#### <a name="submission_iomerge_files_expected"></a>![Optional](https://img.shields.io/badge/Optional-yellow) Property `Config > submission > iomerge > files_expected`

**Title:** IO-merge file expectations

|                           |                                                                             |
| ------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Type**                  | `object`                                                                    |
| **Additional properties** | ![Any type: allowed](https://img.shields.io/badge/Any%20type-allowed-green) |

**Description:** Number of files expected for merge. Set to 0 to let the code decide and -1 for no checking.

| Property                                                 | Pattern | Type   | Definition | Title/Description                                                              |
| -------------------------------------------------------- | ------- | ------ | ---------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| - [history](#submission_iomerge_files_expected_history ) | No      | number | -          | History files, 0 implies -1 as the number of files is highly output dependent. |
| - [surfex](#submission_iomerge_files_expected_surfex )   | No      | number | -          | Surfex files, 0 implies nproc_io files                                         |
| - [fullpos](#submission_iomerge_files_expected_fullpos ) | No      | number | -          | Fullpos files, 0 implies 1+nproc_io files                                      |

| ----------- | -------- |
| **Type**    | `number` |
| **Default** | `-1`     |

**Description:** History files, 0 implies -1 as the number of files is highly output dependent.
